Scene 439 of The Entire History of Apple: 16:9 widescreen minimalist 2D cartoon webcomic illustration on off-white (#FAF9F6) background with a soft, hand-drawn charcoal and watercolor brush vignette border around the edges. Visual Concept: A classic artisan workbench with tools neatly arranged, under a warm swinging lamp, as the craftsman waves goodbye into the soft vignette. Characters: Minimalist 2D cartoon stick figures with solid white filled circular heads, simple expressive curved black ink eyes and mouths, dark thin single-line stick limbs, wearing authentic era-appropriate clothing. Environment: Detailed, atmospheric 2D background in muted cinematic earth tones (warm amber lamplight, vintage beige, industrial grey, brick red, aged paper cream) with clean comic line art, subtle cross-hatching, and zero glossy 3D rendering. In-Image Label: Distinct hand-lettered comic text in bold quotes in upper frame reading "SEE YOU AT THE NEXT BUILD". Composition: Cinematic 16:9 landscape framing, perfectly balanced for smooth Ken Burns camera pan and zoom.
